Let's think about active-set / LP approach from problem statement. They propose: Given f normalized sum=√(2n), M=||f*f||∞. Solve LP maximizing sum g subject to g≥0 and ||f*g||∞≤M. Then rescale, mix f←(1-t)f+t g. This is like a linearization: The feasible set for new g in linear constraints based on current f. Since objective for g alone not directly, but moving towards g that has low convolution with f. If iterate, could converge to fixed point where best g with f*f≤M is f itself? Maybe can improve.

We can implement this LP for moderate n using cvxpy? But n=4806, constraints 2n-1~9611, variables 4806; LP with sparse matrix. Solvers maybe GLOP/PDLP? We have cvxpy with many solvers but maybe only one CPU; could be slow. But we can implement the LP more directly? The LP: max 1^T g s.t. A g ≤ M 1, g≥0, where A is Toeplitz (conv f*g). A_{m,j}=f[m-j] if valid. M normalized? We can scale sum. This is a linear program with dense-ish banded? A has at most n nonzeros per column? Actually for each j, f shifted: support n, so A is 2n x n dense triangular (about 2n^2/2 entries ~ n^2=23M for n=4806). Too big but maybe sparse? It's block? Could solve with scipy.optimize.linprog? Too slow. But we can compute dual explicitly? Primal max c^T g s.t. A g ≤ M, g≥0. Dual min M 1^T y s.t. A^T y ≥ 1, y≥0. A^T y at j = ∑_m A_{m,j} y_m = (f * y)_j (full convolution). So dual: minimize M ∑ y_m subject to (f*y)[j] ≥1 for j=0..n-1, y_m≥0. This is a linear program on y (length 2n-1) with constraints n. Symmetric. Still LP but maybe easier with simple structure. The primal optimum sum g equal min ∑y. The active set method might be heavy.

But maybe we can use the dual to derive a multiplicative update: The LP solution for g can be found by linear programming; but we can approximate via projected dual ascent.

Let's write fast_score:
```
def _fft_size(target):
    size=1
    while size<target: size <<=1
    return size

def fast_convolution(p):
    n=p.shape[0]
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
    f=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, size)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    # deal with tiny imaginary? irfft real
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-300]=0
    return c

def fast_score_normalized(p):
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return np.inf, fast_convolution(p)
    q=p/s
    c=fast_convolution(q)
    return 2.0*q.size*c.max(), c
```
For large n, c.max maybe. Could avoid normalizing each time if maintain sum=1. But for random, normalize.

Cross-correlation weights:
```
def cross_weights(p, weights):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size < n+L-1: size <<=1 # 3n-2
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    r=np.zeros(L)
    r[:] = weights[::-1]
    fr=np.fft.rfft(r,size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fp*fr, size)
    g=conv[L-1 : L-n : -1]? Need proper slicing.
```

Update function:
```
def multiplicative_update(p, g, eta):
    # scale g to avoid overflow
    gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
    if gmax <=0: return p
    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300) - eta * (g/gmax)
    logp -= np.max(logp)
    q=np.exp(logp)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return p
    return q/s
```
But g may be all positive? Could. We want decrease high g. If g large for coordinates, p decreases. Since weights normalized, g roughly weighted average of p shifts; scale gmax maybe max p times active count.

Projection onto simplex (nonnegative sum 1):
```
def project_simplex(v):
    x=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=x.size
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1))-cssv>0)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(x-theta,0)
```
This handles no upper bound. Values sum 1. Good.

Let's design one efficient loop:
```
def optimize_branch(parent, budget, seed):
    rng=...
    p=normalize(parent)
    current, conv = score_conv(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best=current
    eta=1.0; tau_frac=0.1
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time < deadline and no_improve < limit:
       if conv is None: conv=...
       maxv=conv.max()
       # choose tau based on anneal: tau_frac = max(0.02, 0.2 * (1 - it/anneal) + random)
       x=(conv-maxv)/tau
       x-=x.max(); w=np.exp(x); w|=w.sum()
       g=cross_weights(p,w)
       h=p*g
       hmax=np.max(h)
       if hmax>0:
          # eta decay, maybe occasional random
          eta = eta_base * (0.5 if random<0.5 else 2)
          logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - eta * (h/hmax)
          logp-=logp.max()
          q=np.exp(logp)
          s=q.sum()
          if not finite: q=p
          else: q=q/s; q[np.where(q<1e-300)]=1e-300; q/=q.sum()
       else: break
       current, conv = score_conv(q)
       if current < best - 1e-12:
           best=q.copy(); best_p=p; no_improve=0; eta*=1.05
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve == 50: eta *=0.7
           if no_improve > 500:
               # perturb best
               p = best_p.copy()
               p *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,n))
               p=normalize(p)
               current,conv=score_conv(p)
               no_improve=0
           # else keep p as current? We used q even if current worse; could revert to previous p.
```

The LP approach: Starting f normalized sum=√(2n) (not sum1). M=||f*f||∞. Solve max sum b s.t. b≥0, ||f*b||∞≤M. Then rescale b sum√(2n), mix. def normalize_nonnegative(seq):
    arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    return arr/s
```

`sqrt_prefix`:
```
def sqrt_prefix(n):
    a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
    a/=a.sum(); return a
```
Loop 4806 negligible.

Power family:
```
def power_family(n, alpha):
    x=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    a=x**(-alpha)
    a/=a.sum(); return a
```

def mix_arrays(u, v, t):

    if p.sum()<... : return None
    p = p/ p.sum()
    return p

```
x=(conv-maxv)/tau; x-=x.max(); w=np.exp(x); w/=w.sum()
g=cross_weights(p,w); h=p*g; hmax=np.max(h)
for cand_eta in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.125]:
   if cand_eta<=0: break
   logp=np.log(p+1e-300)
   logp = logp - cand_eta * (h/hmax)
   logp -= logp.max()
   q=np.exp(logp)
   s=q.sum()
   if s==0 or not finite: continue
   q/=s
   # floor
   q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
   if q.sum()<0.99: q = q + 1e-300; q/=q.sum()
   qs, qconv = score_conv(q)
   if qs < current - 1e-15:
      return q, qs, qconv, cand_eta
return p,current,conv,eta*0.5 # reduce
```

So cross_weights code:
```
def cross_weights(p, weights):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    size=1
    target=n+L-1
    while size<target: size <<=1
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    r=np.zeros(size)
    r[:L]=weights[::-1]
    fr=np.fft.rfft(r,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fr,size)
    g=c[L-n:L][::-1]
    return g
```
But `weights[::-1]` length L. Good.
Need weights normalized? Not necessary, scale affects eta; but w sum1 to control.

Could compute weights more stably:
```
def softmax_weights(conv, maxv, tau):
    if tau <= 0: return one-hot at argmax
    x = conv / tau
    x -= x.max()
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0: return one-hot
    return w/s
```
Using x-max avoids under/overflow. Since conv max maybe small (1e-4), tau maybe 1e-5, x ~40 max? Good.

Let's implement a `block_sequence(n, widths, amps)`:
```
widths = np.asarray(widths)/np.sum(widths)
amps = np.asarray(amps)
p=np.empty(n)
starts = np.floor(np.cumsum(widths)*n).astype(int) ; append n
for idx: p[starts[idx]:starts[idx+1]] = amps[idx]
p/=p.sum(); return p
```

- For K in [3,4,5,6]: random widths Dirichlet(K, α=1 or 2), random amps lognormal. Score, keep best. Nelder-Mead from best.

Suppose we allocate mass in blocks of decreasing sizes (like a Cantor distribution) with amplitudes chosen so convolution at each scale balanced. This can make max convolution ∑ contributions across scales, while sum grows faster than one singular.

We can choose distance and scales to balance self-peaks and cross-peak. This is like constructing a sequence with a few clusters.

Cluster model: components i with amplitude m_i (sum), width w_i, located at positions s_i. Self-convolution peak of component i ~m_i^2/w_i. Cross peak between i,j ~m_i m_j / min(w_i,w_j) at lag s_i-s_j. We want all peaks ≤C; maximize total mass M=∑m_i subject to total length. For large n, widths maybe large relative to overlaps. This is a linear problem in masses: Constraints m_i^2/w_i ≤C (quadratic) and m_i m_j / w_{min} ≤C. If we set all components with same mass/width ratio? Let define r_i = m_i /√w_i (self sqrt). Cross constraint m_i m_j /w_min = (m_i/√w_i)(m_j/√w_j)*√(w_max/w_min). If w_i comparable, cross ≤(r_i r_j). To balance, choose r_i ~√C. Then m_i~√(C w_i). Sum M~√C∑√w_i. With total width n, by Cauchy M maximized by putting all width in one component, but then cross none; self F=2n*C? If one component width W, m=~√(CW), objective F=2n*m^2? normalized? Let's use unnormalized max=C, sum M. F=2nC/M^2 (if sum not normalized? Actually max conv C, sum M, F=2nC/M^2). For one component width W<<n, M^2=C W, F=2nC/(C W)=2n/W, large unless W=n. For uniform W=n, M^2=Cn, F=2. Need many components spread over length but cross constraints. If cross constraints force r_i r_j bounded, total M could be ∑√w_i. For fixed total width n, this is maximized by one component; but cross with others? If one big and many small, cross big-small may be limited if small width? Let's not.

def random_block_sequence(n, K, rng):
    widths = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K)*0.5)
    logs = rng.normal(0,1,K)
   amps=np.exp(logs)
    return block_sequence(n,widths,amps)

Detailed optimize loop:
```
def optimize_sequence(parent, deadline, branch_id=0, print_progress=False):
    p=_normalize(parent)
    if p is None: return parent, np.inf
    n=p.size
    current, conv = _score_conv(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best=current
    rng=np.random.default_rng(1234+branch_id)
    eta = 1.0 if branch_id==1 else (0.2 if branch_id==0 else 5.0)
    tau_base = 0.1
    sigma=0.05
    no_improve=0
    stagnant=0
    print_progress=False
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if print_progress and time-last_print>30: print; last_print=...
        maxv=conv.max()
        if maxv <=0: break
        # anneal tau based on no_improve/iters:
        progress = min(1.0, iters/20000)
        tau_frac = 0.3 + (0.02 -0.3)*progress
        tau_frac *= rng.uniform(0.7,1.4)
        weights = softmax_weights(conv, maxv*tau_frac)
        g=cross_weights(p, weights)
        h=p*g
        hmax=np.max(h)
        if not finite or hmax<=0: q=p; qs=current
        else:
            logp=np.log(p+1e-300)
            hh=h/hmax
            accepted=False
            for eta_try in (eta, eta*0.4, eta*0.16):
                if eta_try <= 0: continue
                logp_try = logp - eta_try*hh
                logp_try -= np.max(logp_try)
                q=np.exp(logp_try)
                s=q.sum()
                if s>0 and np.isfinite(s):
                    q/=s
                    # floor and renormalize
                    q=np.where(q>1e-300, q, 1e-300)
                    q/=q.sum()
                    qs, qconv = _score_conv(q)
                    if qs < current - 1e-15:
                        # accept
                        p=q; current=qs; conv=qconv; eta=max(eta, eta_try*0.9)
                        if qs < best - 1e-12:
                            best=qs; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
                        else:
                            no_improve += 1
                        accepted=True
                        break
            if accepted:
                stagnant=0
                # gradually reduce eta
                eta *= 0.9995
                if eta < 1e-4: eta=1e-4
            else:
                stagnant += 1
                eta *= 0.8
                if stagnant > 100:
                    # random perturbation from best
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    sigma *= 1.2
                    if sigma>1.0: sigma=1.0
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                    p = p * np.exp(noise)
                    p = _normalize(p)
                    current, conv = _score_conv(p)
                    if current < best:
                        best=current; best_p=p.copy()
                    stagnant=0; no_improve=0
                    eta = rng.uniform(0.5,5.0)
                    sigma = min(1.0, sigma*0.5?)
        # if no improvement for long and current much worse than best, reset
        if no_improve > 5000 and current > best*1.05:
            p=best_p.copy()
            current=best; conv=_fft_conv(p)
            no_improve=0
    return best_p, best
```

This may be too line-search heavy: accepted step requires candidate score. If accepted, we compute qs; if not, up to 3. In stagnation, random.

    # evaluate all with fast_score

Implement `project_simplex` carefully. For vector sum not 1, it projects to sum1. If q has large negative, okay.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    x=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=x.size
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u*(np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)) - cssv > 0
    if not cond.any():
        return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(x-theta,0.0)
```
This returns sum1.

Additive step size: h/hmax in [0,1]. eta same. `q = p - eta*hh`. For eta small, p stays. Project.

For multiplicative, after exp, floor:
```
def _multiplicative_update(p, logp, hh, eta):
    l = logp - eta*hh
    l -= np.max(l)
    q = np.exp(l)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None, 1e300
    q /= s
    # If some zeros, add eps if all zeros? But if some underflow to0, fine; nonnegative allowed. However log of zeros next iter problematic. We can enforce a tiny floor relative to sum to maintain positive for logarithmic updates.
    minv = 1e-300 / p.size? If add floor, changes sum negligible.
    zeros=(q<1e-300)
    if zeros.any():
       q[zeros]=1e-300
       s=q.sum(); q/=s
    return q
```

def _random_block_sequence(n,K,rng):
    widths=rng.dirichlet(np.full(K,0.7))
    logs=rng.normal(0.0,1.2,K)
    amps=np.exp(logs)
    return _block_sequence(n,widths,amps)
```

def _modulated_sqrt(n, split, boost):
   u=sqrt_prefix(n)
   m=np.ones(n)
   idx=int(round(split*n))
   if 0<idx<n: m[idx:]=boost
   return u*m
```

Sinusoid:
```
def _sinusoid(base, cycles, amp, phase):
   n=base.size; k=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   p=base*(1+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*cycles*k/n+phase))
   p=np.where(p>0,p,0)
   if p.sum()<=0: return None
   return p
```

Multiplicative update floor:
```
def _multiplicative_update(logp, hh, eta):
    l = logp - eta*hh
    l -= np.max(l)
    q = np.exp(l)
    s = q.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: return None
    q = q / s
    floor = 1e-12 / q.size
    mask = q < floor
    if np.any(mask):
        q[mask] = floor
        s = q.sum()
        if s <=0 or not finite: return None
        q = q / s
    return q
```
Need `logp` not None. `hh` array [0,1].

def _additive_update(p, hh, eta):

Polish function:
```
def _polish_sequence(p, deadline, seed):
   p=_normalize(p); current, conv=score; best_p/best; rng; sigma=0.05; no_improve=0
   n=p.size; size=...
   while time<deadline:
      if n<50? break
      if rng.random()<0.6:
         width=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//100)+1))
         start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
         q=p.copy(); q[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, width))
      else:
         q=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.5, n))
      q=_normalize(q)
      if q is None: continue
      qs,_=_score_conv(q,size)
      if qs < current - 1e-15:
          p=q; current=qs; no_improve=0; sigma*=1.1; if sigma>1: sigma=1; if qs<best: update
      else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>100: sigma*=0.7; no_improve=0
          if no_improve > 1000 or current > best*1.1: reset p=best_p; current=best; no_improve=0; sigma=min(1,sigma*1.5)
   return best_p,best
```

```python
"""
Heuristic search over non-negative probabilities normalized to sum 1.

The objective is scale invariant (without the 1000 clipping), so the search
works mainly with sequences of sum 1.  Candidates are generated from several
parametric families: truncated (1-z)^(-1/2) prefixes, powers, asymmetric
mixtures with their reverses, modulated prefixes, sinusoids, random
exponentials, and random block sequences.

The local optimizer minimizes a smooth log-sum-exp surrogate of max(p*p) using
multiplicative or additive updates.  The gradient direction is the weighted
convolution of p with softmax weights over the current convolution lags.
Multiple branches, random restarts, and final block polishing are used within
the time budget.  The best sequence found by the fast FFT evaluator is
returned, with a small final safety check through evaluate_sequence when
available and affordable.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(x: int) -> int:
    x = int(max(1, x))
    y = 1
    while y < x:
        y <<= 1
    return y


def _normalize(arr) -> "np.ndarray | None":
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _fft_convolution(p: np.ndarray, size: int) -> np.ndarray:
    n = p.size
    target = 2 * n - 1
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, size)
    c = c[:target]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score_convolution(p: np.ndarray, size: int = 0):
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    target = 2 * n - 1
    if size is None or size == 0:
        size = _next_pow2(target)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, size)
    c = c[:target]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))), c


def _sqrt_prefix(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2 * k - 1) / (2 * k)
    a /= a.sum()
    return a


def _power_family(n: int, alpha: float) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(x, -alpha)
    a /= a.sum()
    return a


def _mix_two(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray, t: float) -> np.ndarray:
    p = t * a + (1.0 - t) * b
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return a
    return p / s


def _modulated_sqrt(n: int, split: float, boost: float) -> np.ndarray:
    u = _sqrt_prefix(n)
    m = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = int(round(float(split) * float(n)))
    if 0 < idx < n:
        m[idx:] = float(boost)
    return u * m


def _sinusoid(base: np.ndarray, cycles: float, amp: float, phase: float) -> np.ndarray:
    n = base.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    k = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = base * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * cycles * k / float(n) + phase))
    p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return p / s


def _block_sequence(n: int, widths: np.ndarray, amps: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    widths = np.asarray(widths, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    amps = np.asarray(amps, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if widths.size != amps.size or widths.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    widths = np.where(np.isfinite(widths), widths, 0.0)
    widths = np.maximum(widths, 0.0)
    ws = float(widths.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    widths = widths / ws
    amps = np.where(np.isfinite(amps), amps, 0.0)
    amps = np.maximum(amps, 0.0)
    as_ = float(amps.sum())
    if as_ <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    cum = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(widths)))
    cuts = np.floor(cum * float(n)).astype(np.int64)
    cuts[0] = 0
    cuts[-1] = n
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(widths.size):
        a = int(cuts[i])
        b = int(cuts[i + 1])
        if b > a:
            p[a:b] = amps[i]
    return p


def _random_block_sequence(n: int, k: int, rng) -> np.ndarray:
    widths = rng.dirichlet(np.full(k, 0.8, dtype=np.float64))
    logs = rng.normal(0.0, 1.2, k)
    amps = np.exp(logs)
    return _block_sequence(n, widths, amps)


def _softmax_weights(conv: np.ndarray, maxv: float, tau: float) -> np.ndarray:
    if maxv <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxv):
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    if tau <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        return w
    x = (conv - maxv) / tau
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(conv, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(conv))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _cross_weights(p: np.ndarray, weights: np.ndarray, size: int) -> np.ndarray:
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    r = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    r[:L] = weights[::-1]
    fr = np.fft.rfft(r, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fr, size)
    return c[L - n:L][::-1]


def _project_simplex(v: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * (np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) - cssv) > 0.0
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    return np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)


def _multiplicative_update(logp: np.ndarray, hh: np.ndarray, eta: float):
    l = logp - eta * hh
    l = l - float(np.max(l))
    q = np.exp(l)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    q = q / s
    floor = 1e-12 / float(q.size)
    mask = q < floor
    if np.any(mask):
        q[mask] = floor
        s = float(np.sum(q))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return None
        q = q / s
    return q


def _additive_update(p: np.ndarray, hh: np.ndarray, eta: float) -> np.ndarray:
    q = p - eta * hh
    return _project_simplex(q)


def _optimize_sequence(parent, deadline: float, branch_id: int = 0,
                       additive: bool = False, eta0: float = 1.0,
                       tau0: float = 0.25, tau1: float = 0.03,
                       sigma0: float = 0.10):
    p = _normalize(parent)
    if p is None:
        return parent, np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n < 2 or n > 25000:
        return p, _score_convolution(p)[0]

    local_start = time.monotonic()
    duration = max(1.0, float(deadline) - local_start)
    size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    cross_size = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    current, conv = _score_convolution(p, size)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best = current
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1000 + int(branch_id) * 17)

    eta = float(eta0)
    sigma = float(sigma0)
    no_improve = 0
    stagnant = 0
    iters = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        iters += 1
        maxv = float(np.max(conv))
        if maxv <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxv):
            break

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - local_start) / duration)
        tau_frac = float(tau0) + (float(tau1) - float(tau0)) * progress
        tau_frac *= float(rng.uniform(0.75, 1.35))
        tau_frac = float(np.clip(tau_frac, 0.005, 1.0))
        w = _softmax_weights(conv, maxv, maxv * tau_frac)
        g = _cross_weights(p, w, cross_size)
        h = p * g
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        hh = h / hmax
        logp = None if additive else np.log(p + 1e-300)

        accepted = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            e = eta * factor
            if e <= 1e-13:
                continue
            if additive:
                q = _additive_update(p, hh, e)
                if q is None:
                    continue
            else:
                q = _multiplicative_update(logp, hh, e)
                if q is None:
                    continue
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(q))
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            q = q / s
            qs, qconv = _score_convolution(q, size)
            if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < current - 1e-15:
                current = qs
                p = q
                conv = qconv
                accepted = True
                if qs < best - 1e-12:
                    best = qs
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
                break

        if accepted:
            stagnant = 0
            eta *= 0.999
            if eta < 1e-5:
                eta = 1e-5
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta *= 0.8
            if eta < 1e-5:
                eta = 1e-5
            if stagnant > 80:
                p = best_p.copy()
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                p = p * np.exp(noise)
                p = _normalize(p)
                if p is None or p.size != n:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                current, conv = _score_convolution(p, size)
                if current < best:
                    best = current
                    best_p = p.copy()
                stagnant = 0
                no_improve = 0
                eta = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 5.0))
                sigma = min(1.0, sigma * 1.25)

        if no_improve > 3000 and current > best * 1.02:
            p = best_p.copy()
            current = best
            conv = _fft_convolution(p, size)
            no_improve = 0

    return best_p, best


def _polish_sequence(p0, deadline: float, seed: int = 7):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return p0, np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return p, _score_convolution(p)[0]

    size = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    current, _ = _score_convolution(p, size)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best = current
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    sigma = 0.05
    stuck = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if rng.random() < 0.65:
            width = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 80 + 1)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            end = min(n, start + width)
            q = p.copy()
            q[start:end] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, end - start))
        else:
            q = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * sigma, n))

        q = _normalize(q)
        if q is None:
            continue
        qs, _ = _score_convolution(q, size)
        if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < current - 1e-15:
            current = qs
            p = q
            stuck = 0
            sigma = min(1.0, sigma * 1.08)
            if qs < best:
                best = qs
                best_p = p.copy()
        else:
            stuck += 1
            if stuck > 100:
                sigma = max(0.01, sigma * 0.75)
                stuck = 0
            if stuck > 1500 or (current > best * 1.08 and stuck > 500):
                p = best_p.copy()
                current = best
                stuck = 0
                sigma = min(1.0, sigma * 1.3)

    return best_p, best


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, max_len: int = 20000):
    p = _normalize(arr)
    if p is None or p.size == 0 or p.size > max_len:
        return
    score, _ = _score_convolution(p)
    if np.isfinite(score):
        cands.append((score, p))


def _candidate_score(item):
    return item[0]


def _abs_log_ratio(a: int, b: int) -> float:
    a = max(1, int(a))
    b = max(1, int(b))
    return abs(float(np.log(a)) - float(np.log(b)))


def _is_duplicate(arr, starts):
    for s in starts:
        if s is arr:
            return True
    return False


def _get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    return _normalize(raw)


def _generate_candidates(gen_deadline: float, seed: int):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands = []
    parent = _get_parent_sequence()

    if parent is not None and parent.size <= 20000:
        _add_candidate(cands, parent)
        for sig in (0.05, 0.15, 0.30):
            _add_candidate(cands, parent * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, parent.size)))

    n_parent = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 4806
    if not (64 <= n_parent <= 20000):
        n_parent = 4096

    sizes = []
    for s in (n_parent, 4096, 2048, 1024, 8192):
        if 64 <= int(s) <= 20000 and int(s) not in sizes:
            sizes.append(int(s))
    if not sizes:
        sizes = [4096]
    sizes = sizes[:4]

    for n in sizes:
        if time.monotonic() > gen_deadline:
            break

        u = _sqrt_prefix(n)
        ur = u[::-1].copy()
        _add_candidate(cands, u)

        for t in (0.55, 0.62, 0.70, 0.80, 0.90):
            _add_candidate(cands, _mix_two(u, ur, t))

        for alpha in (0.42, 0.46, 0.49, 0.50, 0.51):
            _add_candidate(cands, _power_family(n, alpha))

        for split in (0.25, 0.40, 0.55, 0.70):
            for boost in (1.2, 1.5, 2.0):
                _add_candidate(cands, _modulated_sqrt(n, split, boost))

        for cycles in (2, 3, 5, 8):
            for amp in (0.25, 0.45, 0.65):
                for phase in (0.0, 1.57079632679, 3.14159265359):
                    _add_candidate(cands, _sinusoid(u, cycles, amp, phase))

        for _ in range(3):
            _add_candidate(cands, rng.exponential(1.0, n))

        for k in (3, 5):
            for _ in range(5):
                _add_candidate(cands, _random_block_sequence(n, k, rng))

    if not cands:
        fallback = _sqrt_prefix(4096)
        cands.append((_score_convolution(fallback)[0], fallback))

    cands.sort(key=_candidate_score)
    return cands


def _safe_evaluate(p) -> float:
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    arr = arr / s
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr]))
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    return val


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s: float = 1500.0, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    final_deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget_s - 3.0)

    gen_time = max(20.0, min(180.0, 0.10 * budget_s))
    gen_deadline = min(t0 + gen_time, final_deadline - 60.0)

    cands = _generate_candidates(gen_deadline, int(seed))
    cands.sort(key=_candidate_score)

    best_p = cands[0][1]
    best_score = cands[0][0]
    print(f"initial best fast score: {float(best_score):.10f} length={best_p.size}", flush=True)

    starts = []
    for score, p in cands[:6]:
        if len(starts) >= 3:
            break
        if not _is_duplicate(p, starts):
            starts.append(p)

    if len(starts) < 3:
        fallback_n = best_p.size
        starts.append(_sqrt_prefix(fallback_n))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 991)
    starts.append(rng.exponential(1.0, starts[0].size))
    if len(starts) < 5:
        starts.append(_sqrt_prefix(starts[0].size))

    branch_specs = [
        {"additive": False, "eta0": 1.0,  "tau0": 0.25, "tau1": 0.04, "sigma0": 0.10},
        {"additive": False, "eta0": 3.0,  "tau0": 0.10, "tau1": 0.02, "sigma0": 0.20},
        {"additive": True,  "eta0": 0.25, "tau0": 0.45, "tau1": 0.08, "sigma0": 0.05},
        {"additive": False, "eta0": 0.4,  "tau0": 0.35, "tau1": 0.05, "sigma0": 0.35},
    ]

    # Main multi-branch local search.
    branch_starts = starts[:4]
    num_branches = max(1, len(branch_starts))
    available = max(120.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())
    branch_budget = max(30.0, min(280.0, available / float(num_branches)))

    for i, p0 in enumerate(branch_starts):
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 90:
            break
        spec = branch_specs[i % len(branch_specs)]
        dl = time.monotonic() + branch_budget
        try:
            p_opt, s_opt = _optimize_sequence(
                p0,
                dl,
                branch_id=i,
                additive=bool(spec["additive"]),
                eta0=float(spec["eta0"]),
                tau0=float(spec["tau0"]),
                tau1=float(spec["tau1"]),
                sigma0=float(spec["sigma0"]),
            )
        except Exception:
            p_opt, s_opt = p0, best_score

        if p_opt is not None and np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
            best_p = p_opt
            best_score = s_opt
            print(f"branch {i} improved best fast score: {float(best_score):.10f} length={best_p.size}", flush=True)

    # Extra focus on the global best.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 80:
        spec = branch_specs[3]
        extra = min(220.0, max(20.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0))
        try:
            p_opt, s_opt = _optimize_sequence(
                best_p,
                time.monotonic() + extra,
                branch_id=50,
                additive=bool(spec["additive"]),
                eta0=float(spec["eta0"]) * 0.5,
                tau0=float(spec["tau0"]) * 0.6,
                tau1=float(spec["tau1"]) * 0.6,
                sigma0=float(spec["sigma0"]),
            )
        except Exception:
            p_opt, s_opt = best_p, best_score

        if p_opt is not None and np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
            best_p = p_opt
            best_score = s_opt
            print(f"extra focus improved best fast score: {float(best_score):.10f} length={best_p.size}", flush=True)

    # Short random/block polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 45:
        polish_time = min(60.0, max(5.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0))
        try:
            p_pol, s_pol = _polish_sequence(best_p, time.monotonic() + polish_time, seed=int(seed) + 31337)
        except Exception:
            p_pol, s_pol = best_p, best_score

        if p_pol is not None and np.isfinite(s_pol) and s_pol < best_score:
            best_p = p_pol
            best_score = s_pol
            print(f"polish improved best fast score: {float(best_score):.10f} length={best_p.size}", flush=True)

    # Final safety / true evaluate when affordable.
    eval_candidates = [best_p]
    for score, p in cands[:5]:
        if len(eval_candidates) >= 6:
            break
        if p is not best_p and not _is_duplicate(p, eval_candidates):
            eval_candidates.append(p)

    eval_deadline = min(final_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, min(70.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())))
    evaluated_best = False

    chosen = best_p
    chosen_val = best_score

    for p in eval_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= eval_deadline:
            break
        if p is None:
            continue
        if p.size > 8000 and time.monotonic() > eval_deadline - 10:
            continue
        val = _safe_evaluate(p)
        if p is best_p:
            evaluated_best = True
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < chosen_val:
            chosen_val = val
            chosen = p
            print(f"true evaluation improved chosen score: {float(chosen_val):.10f} length={chosen.size}", flush=True)

    if not evaluated_best:
        chosen = best_p
        chosen_val = best_score

    final = np.asarray(chosen, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.maximum(final, 0.0)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        final = _sqrt_prefix(4096)
        s = float(final.sum())
    final = final / s

    print(f"final fast/true chosen score: {float(chosen_val):.10f} length={final.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
